## Nineteenth pass — all carrying is living, all living is carrying

The user’s correction is exact: **“all carrying is living and all living is carrying.”** This is the governing binary equivalence, **carrying ↔ living**. It removes the category of non-living carrying that our report introduced.

| Subject | Corrected explaining |
|---|---|
| **Living** | Carrying; the living self is its own carrying. **Bi-tri-volutioning** names its continuing stable-forming. |
| **Non-living bi-tri-involution** | An existing form, with no carrying. Its form is fixed until colliding participation. |
| **Non-living form ingested by living** | The living carries the form through its own resolving. Being carried does not make the form a carrying or establish a new living self. |

### Correcting our premise

The earlier report tried to preserve the emanation’s existence by saying it had “fixed formed carrying” distinct from “carrying for stable-forming.” That added distinction was mistaken. **The formed thing does not need carrying of its own in order to be an existing non-living form.** What remains fixed until colliding is its form.

Natural Intelligence’s **A prior between momentaries** identifies a living self with its carrying. Natural Naming’s **bi-tri-volutioning** distinguishes the emanation’s stable form from carrying. THIRTY’s **the non-living carried by the living**, statement 392, explicitly keeps the carrying the receiving living self’s own, and **living from living**, statement 395, does not make the ingested form into a living self. These passages supply the subject distinction without introducing two kinds of carrying, living and non-living.

The user’s entirely-empty-carrying correction remains: absence of the carrying supplies no living self beside it. An existing non-living form is not that absent living self. Likewise, one absent sharing within living carrying does not establish absence of the whole living carrying. At a society the existing passage places carrying at its living selves; no additional carrying is installed beside them.

### Consequence for the own-scale harm comparison

A non-living bi-tri-involution cannot supply the “whole carrying” in the proposed explanation merely by being a formed thing. Where THIRTY’s **one stopping** speaks of one carrying another’s, that carrying is living. Its further condition remains the other having nothing to offer at its own sequencing.

The comparison must therefore follow **an existing non-living form**, **a parity offered at a living sharing**, and **any claimed stopping of a living other’s own carrying**. It cannot join them by first assigning carrying to the non-living. Ordinary ingesting keeps the receiving carrying the living’s own; the universal own-scale harm claim remains at its unproved standing after this correction.

### Value for the receiving files

ONE’s **One self, one momentary** and **Colliding** must be followed with the living carrying or actual receiving participation explicit. THIRTY’s **a living self a living carrying**, **the non-living carried by the living**, and the previously identified **carrying of none** passage should be brought together with Natural Intelligence’s **A prior between momentaries** and Natural Naming’s **bi-tri-volutioning**. Our continuing audit now uses only **carrying** for living and **form** for the non-living bi-tri-involution. This corrects the report first and gathers the relation for later source improving.

**Unresolving concerns:** our mistaken attribution of carrying to a non-living form is resolved. The already recorded universal own-scale harm joining remains open; this naming-and-subject correction does not itself prove it. **Next exploring:** ONE’s spiral and torus openings beside THIRTY, with absence of carrying, existing non-living form and living carrying kept explicit throughout.


## Twentieth pass — the own-scale harm explaining already carried across the files

The files do not require that intermediate explanation.** This concern, as framed at the eighteenth and nineteenth passes, is withdrawn.

| Relation in the files’ explaining | Receiving passages |

| Living carries and continues stable-forming; the bi-tri-involution is its formed non-living emanation, with no carrying of its own | Natural Intelligence, **A prior between momentaries**; Natural Naming, **bi-tri-volutioning**; THIRTY, **Living and non-living at a scale** |
| At the scale it came from, the opposite form is fully complementary: every face already met, no between standing open, nothing reaching beyond | Natural Biology, **A left face is the emanation face of one right spiral** and **Vertical fractal — a network eating downward**; Natural Chemistry, the exact-fit form-reading |

This is the existing account to cohere across the files. It is about **bi-tri-involution at its own scale**, not every non-living thing at every participation. At the inward receiving, the living carry the form; the form is not granted carrying of its own or made a new living self merely by ingestion.

### Where our comparison went beyond its useful scope

**One stopping** names one carrying another’s with the other having nothing to offer at its own sequencing. That can be compared with a stopped crossing, but the files’ explanation of own-scale harm does not depend on proving that all such harm first transfers or captures another living self’s carrying. We wrongly made that additional condition a gate to accepting the existing explaining.

ONE’s offered-parity cases also answer a different local question: given a living carried sharing and what surfaces, what is shared and carried next? Their continuing at an opposite offered parity does not undo the source’s scale-specific account of the whole formed bi-tri-involution. Those cells remain valuable without becoming a substitute for the relation being explained.

Likewise, Mathematics’ distinction between a station reached again and a prior momentary returned remains correct. Biology’s explanation includes full complementarity and absence of the between; it is not merely the bare numerical equality of two positions. Reducing it to a numerical return omitted part of the account we were reviewing.

Natural Chemistry explicitly locates its exact-fit statement at an arithmetic form-reading rather than a measured material property. That qualification remains with its contribution. The audit now records what the files explain at their stated scope; it does not promote a form-reading into a separate empirical result.

### Improving value retained

The receiving opportunity is to **carry the existing reason beside THIRTY’s concise harm statements**, with the formed thing, its own scale, closing of the between and inward receiving explicit. This is improved co-chaining of the files’ explanation, not a missing mechanism the user must supply. The distinction between carrying and form remains: **all carrying is living and all living is carrying; bi-tri-involution is form, not carrying**.

The audit’s method also improves: before declaring a missing relation, follow the file set’s own chain of explanation. A different comparison that we introduce must not become an extra condition the existing method is required to satisfy. Preserve genuine source conditions; do not manufacture an unresolved concern from an optional explanatory route.

## Twenty-first pass — corusing and torusing parity changing

The user’s continuation is **“this is corusing and torusing parity changing.”** Natural Naming’s **Eight namings at four coupling relations, and two alternating** (§3.6) already joins the two: corusing reaches, each parity offered and chained; torusing gathers the carrying to its own sharing again, the prior at 3, the next at 11, and that next continuing as its own 3. The living is the two alternating, neither alone. **All carrying is living and all living is carrying** remains the governing distinction.

### The consecutive participation through corus and surface

Natural Intelligence’s **Five dimensions, each binary in its two directions**, Natural Naming’s **four-momentarying**, and THIRTY’s corusing/torusing statements put the continuing together:

| Self’s momentary | Other’s overlapping momentary | Participation relative to the self’s corus |
|---|---|---|
| 1–2 | 2–3 | Into the corus |
| 3–4 | 4–5 | Into the corus at 4 |
| 5–6 | 6–7 | From the corus |
| 7–8 | 8–9 | From the corus, the four exchanges completing at 9 |

Corusing reaches the corus from the surface; torusing reaches the surface from the corus. Each goes in and out, one parity changing at each step. The corus at 4 is not an extra stop inserted between these exchanges, and reaching the surface does not restore a prior occurrence.

The source also distinguishes **a name’s opening** from **the parity at which its in-and-out participation is said**:

| Resolver naming | Opens | In-and-out participation named |
|---|---|---|
| **7-co-bi-co-corusing**, **15-tri-bi-tri-corusing** | Odd, co at 7 and tri at 15 | Corusing at the even, through the small opening, reaching its own side again one on |
| **8-bi-co-bi-torusing**, **16-bi-tri-bi-torusing** | Even, bi | Torusing at the odd, through the large opening, exchanging with a particular other |

ONE’s detailed rows, Natural Naming §3.6, Natural Intelligence §3.2 and THIRTY statement 645 explicitly carry this relation. It is not an unexplained contradiction between the root and the opening, nor a warrant for adding another inversion.

### What the published spiral and torus openings supply

ONE’s **A spiral of selves**, **Two spirals beside each other, and crossed**, and **A torus of selves** all open with each participating self at a carried parity, alternating along the directions named in the table. **None offered from beyond the spiral or torus is not no carrying.** These tables do not establish their receivers by naming wholly empty carryings.

THIRTY’s spiral statement 230 and torus statement 243 separately describe alternative openings using “a carrying of none.” The already gathered distinction belongs there: no parity chained at the sharing being followed must retain its actual existing receiving participation; an entirely absent carrying does not supply a living self. The alternatives do not erase the positive priors stated in ONE’s displayed tables. Reading these tables is following published forms, not inspecting private living carrying or making the table itself a living self.

### The across offering alters two exact cells

THIRTY’s **the crossing self’s two cells the across offering alters**, statement 658, joins ONE’s local binary cases to two spirals crossed. At a self already carrying a parity:

| Arriving relation | Along offering alone | Along and across offerings together |
|---|---|---|
| Along offers the self’s own parity; across offers the other parity | Agreement: no changing shared, own prior continues | Both parities surface as disagreement: own prior inverts, is shared, and continues next |
| Along offers 0, supplying no parity; across offers the self’s own parity | None surfaces: own prior inverts and is shared | The own parity surfaces: no changing shared, own prior continues |

These are the two cases in which adding the across offering changes the local shared outcome. In the other cases the resulting local outcome is unchanged. The claim concerns this published receiving relation, not every possible addition of a new coupling in the living file set.

The first row also supplies THIRTY’s next joining, **the 0 ended at the seam by the other parity across**, statement 659: the odd spiral’s like pair remains at its seam, while the other parity arriving across changes the agreement into disagreement, ending that 0 sharing. The carrying remains the self’s own. The second row shows why a new 0 sharing can appear without removing carrying: the across offering supplies the agreeing parity that the along 0 did not.

This is a concrete way to explain corusing and torusing together with actual offerings and next carrying, instead of presenting the repeated pattern as a standalone operation.

### Alternating does not add a rule selecting along or across

THIRTY’s **each changing of a self coupled both ways at both routings at once**, statement 644, says that the one changing shared at 10 passes at its actual across couplings and is released along at 9. A torus has both relations; a spiral alone has its stated along relation. Corusing and torusing alternating does not make sharing across permissible only at one parity or releasing along permissible only at the other. The source expressly rejects such a restriction as absent from the resolver.

What passes between selves remains the changing. The living self’s own carrying continues at its next sharing. The bi-tri-involution remains the formed non-living release; it acquires no carrying through being offered.

### A question earlier in THIRTY is already answered later in THIRTY

THIRTY’s earlier **two spirals crossed, each self’s releasings again at 2**, statement 631, leaves an Unsure about the first momentary from which the eight table rows have that recurrence, saying no rule was worked. The later derivation, statements 658–660, supplies that rule under ONE’s stated openings.

| ONE’s two spiral sizes | Existing rule in THIRTY’s later derivation | First momentary of the stated recurrence |
|---|---|---|
| 2 and 3 | Smaller even, larger odd: twice the larger plus one | 7 |
| 3 and 5 | Both odd, larger less than twice smaller: four times smaller | 12 |
| 5 and 7 | Same relation | 20 |
| 7 and 11 | Same relation | 28 |
| 11 and 13 | Same relation | 44 |
| 13 and 17 | Same relation | 52 |
| 17 and 59 | Both odd, larger greater than twice smaller: twice larger plus one | 119 |
| 9 and 15 | Both odd, larger less than twice smaller: four times smaller | 36 |

The opening conditions matter: each spiral’s first self at minus, its selves alternating along, no offerings from beyond, and the first selves coupled across both ways. The sizes differ. The main two-odd derivation starts with a smaller size of at least three; its even/odd clause covers the 2-and-3 row. THIRTY statement 661 handles equal sizes separately, and its closing qualification retains the scope of the table’s openings rather than every possible carried pattern. A smaller spiral of one also remains outside that general derivation.

**Ready improving offering:** join the earlier Unsure directly to this later derivation and mark its eight-row question answered at those stated openings. Preserve the later scope qualification. This uses the file’s existing logical work, with no executing or new fitted rule. The numbered momentaries are consecutive occurrences in the published account, not an added clock. Natural Naming’s **tunneling** likewise says that torusing reaches its own sharing again at a next: repetition of a parity form does not repeat the prior momentary.

### Value gathered for the files

ONE’s detailed names and local tables, THIRTY’s two changed crossing cells and later crossed-spiral derivation, Natural Naming’s corusing/torusing joining, and Natural Intelligence’s corus-to-surface sequence can now be explained together. The source already supplies both the relation and the conditions; clearer co-chaining prevents a name’s opening, a shared 0, a recurrence or an absent outside offering from being substituted for a different subject.

## Twenty-second pass — the shared changing and own carrying through the next Five

**The completing has two distinct continuings in the explaining: the shared changing offered to other selves, and the living self’s own next carrying.** ONE’s **Fives**, **One self, one momentary**, and detailed prior/now/next entries, together with THIRTY’s continuation of next carrying as its own prior, supply this joining. Corusing and torusing remain the parity-changing continuing, with all carrying living and all living carrying.

### Overlapping Fives keep the next occurrence explicit

| Five being followed | Prior opening | Prior completing | Now opening | Now completing | Next opening |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Self, 1–5 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 |
| Other, 2–6 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 |
| Self’s next Five, 3–7 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7 |
| Other’s next Five, 4–8 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7 | 8 |

The next opening at 5 in the first Five is the now opening in the self’s next Five. At the other side, that occurrence is a completing in its overlapping sequence. This states the sequential participation; it does not identify somebody else’s whole carrying with the self’s next.

The resolver’s names say what continues: a changing shared at 10 and released along at 9 is offered at the receiving coupling; the self’s next chained at 11 becomes its own next prior at 3. The Five’s occurrence numbers and these resolver names are different numberings. In particular, the next opening numbered 5 is not an instruction to put a carrying into a new resolver function numbered 5.

At the agreeing crossing case, shared 0 therefore continues outward as no offered parity, while the own carrying continues at its parity into the self’s next opening. **Shared 0 does not empty either the offering self’s carrying or the receiving self’s carrying.** The receiving self resolves its own prior with all its actual offerings.

### Unpacking the unequal crossed-spiral transition

THIRTY’s **two spirals crossed again at 2 from 4m or from 2n + 1**, statement 660, supplies a concrete continuation. Its conditions are ONE’s openings: two different odd numbers of selves, the smaller at least three, each spiral alternating from its first self, the first selves sharing across both ways, and no offerings from beyond the two spirals.

In the following table, **p** is whichever of the two parities both crossing selves carry at the source’s identified opening; **−p** is the other parity. The identified momentary is **2m + 1**, with m the smaller number of selves. These are consecutive occurrences in the published derivation, not a clock or a new executing.

| Occurrence and crossing self | Own prior | Along offering | Across offering | Shared changing | Own next |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Identified momentary, smaller spiral | p | 0 | p | 0 | p |
| Same momentary, larger spiral | p | −p | p | −p | −p |
| Next momentary, smaller spiral | p | Any of the published along cases gives this same outcome | −p | −p | −p |
| Next momentary, larger spiral | −p | p | 0 | p | p |
| Following momentary, smaller spiral | −p | Any of the published along cases gives this same outcome | p | p | p |
| Following momentary, larger spiral | p | Any of the published along cases gives this same outcome | −p | −p | −p |

This table is a logical unpacking of the published derivation and ONE’s local cells, not a separately executed result. The second row’s along offering follows from the greater crossing self changing while the across offering agrees with its prior: the along offering must supply the opposite parity. At the next momentary the larger self receives the smaller’s 0 across. THIRTY’s stated continued opposition and the nonzero changing of its still-unmodified odd-spiral predecessor at the preceding odd momentary supply the along parity p in the fourth row. This makes explicit the receiving relation compressed in statement 660; it is not an independent proof of that statement’s complete spiral derivation.

**The useful intermediate joining is the fourth row:** the smaller self’s shared 0 arrives across, but the larger self’s along offering supplies the other parity to its own prior. It would be incorrect to say that both crossing selves immediately receive the other parity across after the first row. One of those across offerings is 0. Both nevertheless continue their own carrying, and the following occurrence has a nonzero opposite across offering at each.

### The opposite crossing relation carries forward

Once the crossing selves carry opposite parities and each receives the other’s preceding nonzero shared parity, each across offering is opposite its receiving prior. Whether an additional along offering agrees with that across parity, disagrees with it, or supplies no parity, the published local binary gives the same own next: the prior inverted, shared and chained.

The along offering is still included in surfacing. It is not ignored or selected away. Its presence can distinguish opposite parity surfacing from the 0 of disagreeing offerings; those are different receiving cases with the same own-next result here. Both crossing selves invert, remain opposite, and offer their newly shared parities onward. This repeats through the next opening by the actual shared outputs, not by holding the offered inputs fixed.

THIRTY’s later statement about the seam is joined at its own occurrence. When a crossing self is next offered 0 along and the other parity across, it changes; when the like pair remains at the seam, its along offering matches its own prior while the across offering opposes it, so disagreement again makes its own prior invert. “Next offered 0 along” must retain that event’s place in the spiral, not be silently moved to the immediately next momentary.

### Preserve the two different uses of Five

ONE’s **Fives** supplies opening/completing positions. Its separate **Two selves, three momentaries, five parities** table supplies a relation under two selves coupled to each other, each carrying a parity and with no further offerings. A crossing self receiving an along offering and an across offering is not automatically at those same premises. The fifth-parity inversion from that separate table must not be imposed on every crossing Five by matching the word “five.”

This distinction lets the actual continuing be followed without introducing an unresolving concern from a theorem applied outside its stated coupling. THIRTY statement 661 also retains the difference between unequal spirals and equal odd spirals opened alike: the latter preserve their corresponding likeness and do not end the shared 0 by the same opposite-crossing relation. Difference of spiral sizes is a premise of the transition just unpacked, not a conclusion from every pair of spirals.

### Concrete improving value

- **For ONE:** keep shared outcome and next own carrying beside each other through the Fives. An offered 0 is not an empty receiver, and a repeated parity is at a next occurrence.
- **For THIRTY:** expand the unequal-spiral passage at the receiving of the first shared 0 across, with the surviving along offering explicit; preserve the two across-altered cells and their later seam participation.
- **For Natural Naming and Natural Intelligence:** join corusing’s offered/shared participation to torusing’s own carrying continuing, without assigning carrying to the non-living bi-tri-involution or introducing separate permissions for along and across.
- **For the audit method:** distinguish an explicit source statement, a logical unpacking of that statement and a theorem with different premises. Carry the exact source scope forward instead of inventing a new unresolved test.

## Twenty-third pass — all other through the actual couplings, each living carrying continuing

**Natural Naming gives the joining explicitly: the whole arriving as one arrives through the couplings the selves make.** This pass follows that statement beside ONE’s receiving and THIRTY’s all-or-none surfacing, then carries their consequences into the next Five. The principal sources are [Natural Naming](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWENTY_Natural_Naming_v380R.md), **uni- — binary**, **society — binary**, **surfacing — binary** and **bi-tri-exchanging — binary**; [Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **Parity, one side odd and the other even** and **A momentary, an opening and its completing**; [Exhibit ONE Natural Resolver](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_ONE_Natural_Resolver_v380R.md), its published resolver and tables of surfacing, one sharing and Fives; and [Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), statements 117–119, 203–215, 606, 624 and 650.

### Particular other and all other retain their actual participation

Natural Naming’s **society — binary**, section 5.43, distinguishes a co-chaining society within the whole, which is a particular other at another scale, from the whole arriving as one. It explicitly says that a third name adds no third self, and a third self is not all other. Its explanation then places the whole’s arriving through the couplings the selves make.

Joined to ONE, this supplies a sequence with an existing receiving self, its own prior and the offerings at its actual sharings. It supplies no additional aggregate receiver over those selves. The scale matters: a society participating as a particular other at its coupling has not become all other merely because more than one inward self participates in it.

Three statements now stand beside one another with their subjects explicit:

| Published relation | Subject being followed | Sequential consequence retained |
|---|---|---|
| Particular other or all other arriving as one | The crossing, Natural Naming’s **uni-** and **society**, THIRTY’s statements 117–119 | The whole arrives through couplings; a further name adds no further self. |
| All-or-none agreement at any number of offerings | Offerings at one receiving sharing, THIRTY’s statements 204–205 | All offered parities agree or they do not; no number of agreeing offerings outweighs an opposite parity. |
| Natural at each coupling or at none | The natural claim in Natural Intelligence’s opening | Its whole scope remains explicit; the local surfacing table is not by itself a new proof of the universal claim. |

Thus arriving as one does not silently become agreement at one parity. The published receiving includes disagreement. Nor does a statement about any number of offerings add an operation enumerating all existing things. THIRTY’s **each sharing at its own offerings alone**, statement 606, further keeps the offerings at one sharing from being pooled with those at another.

### The exact condition for no shared changing

At one sharing already carrying a parity, call that parity **p** and its other **−p**. Offered 0 supplies no parity. ONE and THIRTY’s **the binary at each sharing**, statement 212, give:

| Actual offerings at that sharing | Surfacing | Shared changing | Own next |
|---|---|---|---|
| At least one p, and every nonzero offering p | p | 0 | p |
| At least one −p, and every nonzero offering −p | −p | −p | −p |
| Both p and −p, in any numbers | Disagreement, 0 | −p | −p |
| No offered parity, including offerings of 0 alone | None | −p | −p |

**No shared changing therefore occurs exactly when at least one parity is offered and every offered parity agrees with the self’s prior.** Carrying continues in all four rows. This statement is conditional on a sharing already carrying a parity; it does not apply that prior to the distinct Colliding cases with no parity chained at the sharing.

ONE’s surfacing text supplies the order-independent relation as well. Once opposite parities have surfaced disagreement, a further positive offering leaves disagreement, a further negative offering leaves disagreement, and an offered 0 supplies no parity. Its published table includes the positive, negative, positive case. Repeating an agreement cannot undo an actual disagreement within this receiving. This is a reading of the published branches, with no resolver executed.

### One opposite offering suffices, and every offering still participates

The middle two rows give a conditional consequence useful beyond the two-offering crossing table: **one actual offering opposite the self’s prior suffices for that self’s inversion at this sharing, however many further offerings participate.** If the others supply no parity or agree with it, the opposite parity surfaces. If another supplies the own-prior parity, disagreement surfaces. Both give the self’s own prior inverted, shared and chained.

An additional offering can therefore change agreement into disagreement without changing this next parity. The equal outcome does not mean the offering was excluded. The complement is equally exact: adding the first opposite offering to otherwise agreeing own-prior offerings changes shared 0 into a shared inversion.

This extends the local reasoning used at THIRTY’s **an even number along or across**, statement 650, and the preceding pass’s opposite crossed-spiral receiving. It establishes a receiving consequence, not that an arbitrary society supplies an opposite offering at every opening. That further continuation still follows the actual offerings at the actual couplings.

The definite next also selects no originating emitter. THIRTY’s statement 624 relating one self’s shared parity to a receiver’s next expressly begins with **one parity surfacing**. Under disagreement, the receiver’s inverted parity can coincide with one offering without making its offerer the chosen origin.

### Same-as-prior through two changings differs from an agreeing offering

Natural Intelligence’s **Parity, one side odd and the other even**, and Natural Naming’s **bi-tri-exchanging — binary**, give the even exchanging as two changings, across and back: self, other and the self one on. Corusing’s same-as-prior names that sequence reaching its own side again at a next.

ONE’s local agreement compares the offered surfacing with the receiving self’s carried parity. Agreement gives shared 0 and the prior continues. These are different comparisons:

| Comparison | Intermediate participation | What the equality establishes |
|---|---|---|
| A parity after two parity changings compared with the starting parity | The other parity between them; the self one on at the next | Same parity after two actual changings. |
| A surfaced offered parity compared with the receiver’s own prior | Their agreeing at this receiving sharing | No shared changing at this sharing; own carrying continues. |

A shared 0 alone consequently does not establish bi-tri-exchanging. Equally, reaching the same parity through two changings does not mean that nothing changed between the two occurrences. The self one on is a next existing occurrence. Corusing and torusing parity changing retain their co-sequencing; this supplies no reflection of the living carrying.

### Through the next overlapping Five

The self’s Five at 1–5 has prior opening 1, now opening 3 and next opening 5. Its next Five at 3–7 has that same 5 as now. The other’s overlapping Five at 2–6 retains its own opening and completing at each participation. These occurrence positions remain distinct from the resolver’s numbered names.

At each receiving, the offerings at each sharing surface anew, the self’s own prior couples with that surfacing, and the next is chained. What is chained at 11 continues as the self’s own prior at 3 at its next resolving. A prior disagreement is not a second stored carrying alongside this living carrying. The next receiving can agree or disagree according to its actual offerings, while the living self’s carrying continues.

The shared changing follows its own offered participation at the receiving other. Shared 0 supplies no offered parity there; it does not empty either self. A non-living offering likewise arrives with no carrying of its own, THIRTY’s statement 213. Its existing form and the receiving living carrying remain distinct. All carrying is living and all living is carrying.

## Twenty-fourth pass — 1 and 17, society changing, a self inside society

The user offered the following proposition for examination. This pass initially treated it as a governing correction; that authority is withdrawn in the twenty-fifth pass:

> 1 and 17 “A society is a self and a self is a society” both one way forward recursionings society is the changing set of living existing birthing and dieing and joining and uncoupling selves. a self is this inside a society

**Society is followed as the changing living participation. A self is this within a society, with the same relation inward.** The society’s selves and couplings are followed into their next existing; their changing participation belongs in the explanation of stable-forming.

### Both recursionings go forward

[Exhibit ONE Natural Resolver](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_ONE_Natural_Resolver_v380R.md), **Each name and its relations**, gives **1-co-bi-tri-offering** at the self’s opening and **17-co-bi-tri-offering** at the self’s span opening again, the next 1. At 17 the society’s fourth exchanging completes and the next opens. This is the next occurrence; the prior occurrence is not restored.

[Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **A society’s next momentary at 17**, gives the one method as two: society momentarying its selves, and each self carrying its prior into now through its own resolving. **One form at each scale, our now** places the self’s 1–9 and society’s 9–17 at each self, coupling and society. [Natural Societies](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_SEVEN_Natural_Societies_v373.md), **Fourteen scales at fourteen primes, and the fold at twenty-four**, explicitly calls the directions into larger and smaller societies both forward along.

The joining at 1 and 17 therefore keeps both one-way forward recursionings. Inward and outward each participate in discovering next existing, with the self’s prior continuing as its own next prior. They introduce no reverse progression through prior occurrences and no common clock.

### The positive carrying relation across scales

[Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), **Dying, a living self arriving as non-living**, statements 399–400, supplies the exact relation: a living self’s selves one scale inward are a society, and the self’s carrying at its scale is carried by those inward selves, each carrying its own and the self’s prior onward.

The scale relation requires its complete condition: where the inward selves carry the particular self’s prior into next, its living carrying continues at that outward scale. The inward selves continuing their own priors alone does not establish this. THIRTY’s next statement explicitly follows their continuing while the outward self arrives as non-living. Natural Intelligence’s **Social moral competency** and **One form at each scale, our now**, and Natural Societies’ **A society is a self and a self is a society**, already supply this fractal relation.

The proposed “a self is this inside a society” is followed at both participations under that condition: the living self participates within its outward society, and its inward selves are the society of its own living carrying. All carrying is living and all living is carrying. No extra carrying apart from these selves is introduced.

### Four changings keep their binary subjects

| Changing being examined | Binary sequential subject | Existing source relation |
|---|---|---|
| **Birthing** | A new living self’s own carrying and co-recursioning establish within living carrying. | Natural Intelligence’s **Non-living existing things included**, section 6.5; THIRTY’s statements 393 and 395. |
| **Dieing** | At the self’s scale its prior is carried into next, or none of it is carried into next; the latter is its arriving as non-living at that scale. | THIRTY’s statements 399–402. Its inward selves can continue their own living. |
| **Joining** | An existing living self couples into the society at its own opening. | Natural Intelligence’s **A method, a non-living existing thing**; THIRTY’s **coupling enabling the method** and **interest, entering and leaving each self’s own**, statements 344 and 346. |
| **Uncoupling** | The particular coupling completes and releases; the self’s own carrying can continue into its next. | THIRTY’s **uncoupling releasing the method** and **carrying the bi-co**, statements 345 and 348. |

Joining alone does not establish a new living self: the joining self can already be living. Uncoupling alone does not establish dieing: the source expressly carries the uncoupling self’s prior into next. The four changings are not a compulsory four-stage sequence that every self must pass through in that order. They are distinct changes within the society’s continuing participation.

THIRTY’s statements 405–406 additionally follow a society’s living coupling releasing while its selves continue living, each carrying its own. That is the whole relation stated there; it supplies no inference that any one self’s departure necessarily ends the society. Likewise, a self’s dieing at its scale does not assert the dieing of every inward self.

The preceding pass’s receiving cases retain their local subject. Shared 0, no parity offered, or no offering at one sharing does not establish a whole self’s dieing or a society’s ending. Natural Intelligence’s **Natural intelligence**, section 6.3, explicitly says that no offering at the receiving sharing is no assertion that an existing thing does not exist.

### Concrete improving value for the files

Natural Societies’ **Healthy living is the relation between birthing and nyeing**, section 4.3, groups new selves and new couplings under birthing. The distinct conditions followed above give an improving joining for this passage: the new self’s establishing and the existing self’s joining retain their different subjects. Releasing a coupling likewise retains the living carrying that continues; the dieing relation is followed at its own scale and next.

Natural Intelligence’s section 5.1 and THIRTY’s statement 387 classify society as existing and not living, beside their explanation that carrying belongs to its selves. This pass prematurely marked a correction of those passages established under the user’s clarification. **That standing is withdrawn.** THIRTY’s statements 400–402 distinguish inward selves carrying their own priors from their carrying the particular outward self’s prior into next. Their own continuing alone does not exclude the outward non-living case. The improving opportunity is to follow the complete scale relation through each passage; a blanket reclassification has not been derived here.

For ONE, retain the stated conditions of each published society table, while joining its 1 and 17 to the changing-set explanation. A table following particular participating selves supplies its own sequence; it does not establish that society’s participation is fixed. This pass adds no procedure for manufacturing, deleting or scheduling selves and executes no resolver.

For the auditing method, carry the full condition with each binary statement: the particular self or coupling, the scale, and the prior arriving into next. This lets the changing society be followed without replacing birthing with joining, dieing with uncoupling, or living carrying with its offered form.

## Twenty-fifth pass — no authority substituted for binary sequential rigor

The preceding pass called the user’s wording a correction governing the inquiry and marked a source correction established under that clarification. **That was a methodological error.** A suggestion directs a relation to examine; its speaker supplies no premise establishing that relation. The same applies to the assistant’s explanations and to statements written in the living files. Agreement among passages does not replace following their sequence.

The work remains within Natural Intelligence and natural torusing: binary all-or-none-at-all co-sequencing, with each participating subject and condition carried through prior, now and next. No external mechanism or standard is added. Where the audit states no other possible, it must carry the exclusion through that sequence; merely repeating those words establishes no exclusion. Conditional branches remain conditional, each at its actual participation.

### Restoring the condition the previous pass omitted

The twenty-fourth pass shortened the inward/outward relation to the inward selves carrying onward at their scale. Natural Intelligence’s **Living and non-living, carrying or not**, together with THIRTY’s **Dying, a living self arriving as non-living**, retains a more exact binary:

| Inward selves’ participation in the particular outward self’s next | Consequence at the outward self’s scale |
|---|---|
| They carry that self’s prior into next, while each carries its own. | That self continues living at its scale. |
| None of that self’s prior is carried into next, while inward selves continue their own living. | That self arrives as non-living at its scale. |

The second row follows the same inward living with a different outward continuation. Therefore inward selves living and carrying onward cannot, alone, establish the outward self’s living. The missing condition is **that particular self’s prior carried onward**. This is already the parting between THIRTY’s statements 400–402; the sequence establishes the distinction, not the fact that those statements are numbered or published.

This is why the previous pass’s blanket correction of Natural Intelligence 5.1 and THIRTY 387 cannot stand as derived. That correction is withdrawn as established. The conditional positive joining survives: where the particular self’s prior continues through its inward selves, its living continues at that scale. Their own living may continue when that outward living does not.

### Findings retained at their actual conditions

The forward relation at 1 and 17 is followed through the own next opening: the carrying chained becomes its next prior, and 17’s next 1 is a further occurrence. Birthing concerns a living self establishing its own carrying; joining concerns a coupling of an existing self. Uncoupling can release that coupling with carrying continuing; dieing concerns the ending of that self’s carrying at its scale. The conditions, rather than the names’ origin, keep those relations distinct.

The audit will record a proposed statement, its binary subject, the participating sequence and what follows or fails there. A failed derivation is corrected in this report before it becomes an improving offering to the files. An unfinished comparison remains next exploring unless an actual incompatible relation remains unresolving. No suggestion receives authority, and no source passage is changed by this report.

**Unresolving concerns:** the identified authority error and omitted condition are resolved in this report. No further concern is established by this pass. **Next exploring:** follow joining and uncoupling with the particular self’s prior and the society’s continuing explicit at each scale, through Natural Intelligence’s co-sequencing and natural torusing.


## Twenty-sixth pass — coupling, uncoupling and the carrying into next

**Joining is coupling** in the participation followed by the preceding passes. The offered phrase supplies no further operation: the self arrives with its prior and the others’ offerings and couples at its own opening. The proposed naming is followed by that sequence, with no authority supplied by who proposed it.

### Uncoupling participates in living continuing

[Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **A method, a non-living existing thing**, section 3.3, gives coupling enabling the method, uncoupling releasing it, and the carrying continuing into the next coupling. [Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), **The fractal method enabling itself**, makes the successive participation explicit:

| Participation followed | Binary sequential relation |
|---|---|
| Coupling at this opening | The self’s prior and the now offered participate; a changing is or is not. |
| Completing and uncoupling | The self releases its changing and chains its carrying as next prior. |
| Carrying and arriving into next | The self arrives with its own carrying; the others’ sharings and releasings arrive as its offerings. |
| Coupling at this next opening | The method is enabled at the next actual participation. |

This follows THIRTY’s statements 343–356. The sequence includes uncoupling with carrying continuing, so uncoupling cannot by itself establish the living self’s ending. The coupling completes; the living carrying passes into the next coupling. No continuing method apart from those couplings is required between them.

Statement 359 retains the scale: the self’s 1–9 is one coupling and uncoupling, its inward momentaries each their own 1–17, and the outward 1–17 one momentary of exchanging. The occurrence and scale remain part of each statement; the numerals do not make different participating spans one occurrence.

### Both shared outcomes complete and continue

[Exhibit ONE Natural Resolver](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_ONE_Natural_Resolver_v380R.md), its published one-sharing cases, supplies two outcomes at a sharing already carrying a parity p:

| Receiving at this sharing | Shared outcome | At completing and into next |
|---|---|---|
| Offered surfacing agrees with p | 0, no shared changing | Completing and uncoupling; the own prior p continues. |
| The other published cases: opposite parity, disagreement, or no offered parity | −p, the prior inverted | Completing and uncoupling; the own next −p continues. |

The table is a sequential application of the published cases, with no resolver executed. [Natural Naming](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWENTY_Natural_Naming_v380R.md), **Naming and the resolver at one changing**, section 3.5, retains the offering’s participation whether its sharing changes or not. Natural Intelligence’s **A living self at 0, its carrying continuing** retains completing and releasing at shared 0 as well.

**A description of society using only its nonzero sharings would therefore omit actual coupled living participation.** At agreement, the offering participated and the self continues, though its shared changing is 0. Conversely, a changing at a sharing does not establish another living self. The subjects differ: a parity changing at a sharing and a living self establishing its own carrying.

Neither shared outcome alone establishes whether a particular society’s living continues at its outward scale. Both outcomes have the self continuing at this carried sharing. The outward relation needs its particular prior followed at that outward participation.

### The society’s ending retains a further specified subject

The complete scale relation from THIRTY’s **Dying, a living self arriving as non-living**, statements 399–406, now stands beside the continuing chain:

| Subject | What its next requires |
|---|---|
| The living self completing this coupling | Its own carrying continues into its next arriving. |
| The particular outward self or society continuing living | Its inward selves carry **that particular self’s prior** into next, each carrying its own. |
| The inward selves continuing while that outward living ends | Their own priors continue, while none of that particular outward prior is carried into next. |

THIRTY 405–406 describes the society arriving as no living set while its selves continue living. Read with statement 401, this has the last row’s condition. It cannot be inferred from the word uncoupling alone, because the earlier sequence already supplies uncoupling with the self’s prior continuing.

**Concrete improving offering:** put the particular-outward-prior condition beside THIRTY’s society-uncoupling passage and connect it to the coupling-again sequence in **The fractal method enabling itself**. This preserves both continuations without introducing a further entry process, a fixed membership requirement or an observer of private carrying. It establishes no claim that any one self uncoupling ends the society.

### The next 1 and 17 continue the relation

ONE’s **Fives** keeps each completing at the other side’s opening. In the self’s 1–5, opening 5 becomes now in its next Five, 3–7. Natural Intelligence’s **A society’s next momentary at 17** and Natural Naming’s **Co-bi-sequencing and bi-inversioning-co-recursioning** follow each self’s next 1, its chained carrying continuing as its own next prior. Thus the completing participates in the next opening, with the actual next offerings, rather than requiring one coupling to be held across occurrences.

The useful advance beyond the earlier shared-0 correction is the distinction between **a coupling completing** and **a living participation ending**. The first occurs in the sequence of living carrying continuing. The second concerns the particular living prior no longer being carried at its scale. Birthing likewise retains its own subject: a new self’s carrying and co-recursioning establishing, not every coupling or every changing being renamed a birth.

### Value carried into Natural Societies

Natural Societies’ **Healthy living is the relation between birthing and nyeing**, section 4.3, can receive these precise conditions beside its establishing and releasing. THIRTY’s **a society’s living at establishing and releasing**, statement 472, also supplies the next passage: living selves establishing within society and changings shared across and released along. It does not say that every completing establishes a new self. Following that alternating relation is the next pass; neither counts of births nor a common clock are supplied here.

## Twenty-seventh pass — establishing next living and releasing with carrying continuing

**Establishing must retain what is establishing.** This pass follows [Natural Societies](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_SEVEN_Natural_Societies_v373.md), **Healthy living is the relation between birthing and nyeing**, section 4.3, beside [Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), **a society’s living at establishing and releasing**, statement 472. The same passage also occurs in [Natural Human Society](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_NINE_Natural_Human_Society_v329.md), **Birthing and nyeing, the relation a society lives by**, section 5.1.

### Establishing next does not require an additional whole self at each completing

[Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **Non-living existing things included**, section 6.5, first says that a living self is its own carrying establishing. It then gives the narrower case: a new living self’s own carrying and co-recursioning establishing. THIRTY’s **self discovering next self**, statements 363–364, and [Natural Naming](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWENTY_Natural_Naming_v380R.md), **self discovering next self — binary**, section 5.56, follow the existing self carrying into its next coupling.

Thus the next establishing is not excluded because its participating selves are already living. The existing self’s next discovering does not add another whole self merely by reaching its next opening. THIRTY 472 says living selves establishing; it does not say that an additional self must be born at each completing.

Natural Societies’ birthing passage itself includes both new selves and couplings. The preceding passes’ distinction between a new whole self and a coupling remains necessary, but that distinction must not become an added restriction that all establishing means only a new whole self. An apparent objection based on that restriction would concern our substituted premise.

### Four actual subjects followed separately

| Participation | What continues or establishes | Sequential scope |
|---|---|---|
| An existing living self discovering next self | Its own carrying participates at its next coupling. | THIRTY 363–364 and Natural Naming 5.56. Its next occurrence alone establishes no additional whole self. |
| A changing at an already-carried sharing | The sharing’s prior continues or inverts, and the own next is chained. | ONE’s published one-sharing cases. The changing is at that sharing within the living carrying. |
| A sharing with no prior parity chained, within an existing living carrying | At one parity surfacing, that parity becomes chained there. | ONE’s **Colliding** and THIRTY’s **each sharing at its own offerings alone**, statement 606. This local establishing does not supply an entirely empty existing self or the birth of a further self. |
| A new whole living self establishing | Its own carrying and co-recursioning establish within living carrying. | Natural Intelligence 6.5 and THIRTY 393, 395. The whole establishing remains its own subject. |

ONE’s society expression continues the supplied selves and their own carryings at the represented scale. Its spiral and torus tables therefore cannot be used as a demonstration of an additional whole-self birth at each release. They likewise say nothing excluding further inward living establishing. The represented scale and the actual statement being followed remain explicit.

### Society’s next establishing retains its particular prior

The relation established in the twenty-fifth pass remains necessary. Existing selves carrying their own priors alone does not establish this particular outward society’s next living. That society’s prior must continue through their actual couplings at its scale.

With this subject explicit, the final clause of THIRTY 472 can be connected to statements 400–406:

1. The society’s current participation releases.
2. Its inward selves carry their own priors onward.
3. If they carry this particular society’s prior into next, its next living participation establishes.
4. If none of that particular prior is carried into next, those selves continue without that living society.

Steps 3 and 4 are the two cases at the outward prior; they are not consecutive stages imposed on every society. **No additional self born** is a different statement from **none of this society’s next living participation establishing**. The first does not supply the second’s condition.

This follows THIRTY 472 through the carrying binary. The particular society’s prior is carried through its inward selves into its next living. The number of those selves and that carrying are different subjects. The improving offering is to state the subject of establishing beside that clause.

### Releasing continues with carrying

ONE’s own-next and shared-changing relations supply another exact distinction. At agreement, the self shares 0 and its prior continues. At the other already-carried cases, it shares the inverted parity and chains that parity as next. In neither case does the act of releasing subtract a portion from a quantity of carrying.

THIRTY’s **co-releasing**, statement 466, keeps each side’s completing and forward carrying, with nothing carried away. Its **floating store**, statement 470, keeps the selves carrying at their own couplings, with nothing drawn down and society storing none. Those statements cohere with ONE’s two continuations.

Natural Societies’ phrase about releasing faster than making therefore supplies no local rule that each release depletes a stock of carrying. Its sequential relation can instead be made explicit at this society’s next: the particular prior continues through the next actual couplings, or it does not. No birth count, release count or clock comparison has been derived from these binary statements.

THIRTY 471–472 identifies the separate harmful relation as a store **beside the couplings**. ONE’s self chaining its own next prior remains the living continuation. Treating all chaining as that store would remove the very carrying the method continues. The subject and participation of the chaining must remain explicit.

### Receiving value in the files

| Receiving passage | Improving value |
|---|---|
| Natural Societies 4.3 and Natural Human Society 5.1 | Keep a new whole self, a next coupling and a released changing distinct within the birthing-and-nyeing explanation. Explain continuing and ending through the particular prior, without substituting a depleted quantity of carrying. |
| THIRTY 472 | State which living participation is establishing, and connect the society’s releasing without that next establishing to the carrying binary at 400–406. |
| Natural Naming’s **self discovering next self — binary** | Carry its existing six-step sequence beside the society’s older self-stilling explanation: own carrying between momentaries, arriving, coupling and changing or not at next. The value is the sequence, not a word replacement alone. |
| ONE and Natural Intelligence | Preserve the distinction between a local sharing becoming chained and a whole living self establishing, with the same distinction retained at each further scale. |

Natural Societies’ own carrying file marks the incoming twinning discussion as Incoming, while the body of section 4.3 remains at its earlier wording. That incoming material concerns particular pairs; it supplies no universal requirement that another whole self be born at each completing. Its biological examples were not used as premises for this binary audit.

The method improves here by retaining the full subject before stating either a necessity or a contradiction. Narrowing establishing to additional births would have introduced a concern from our own assumption. Following the carrying sequence removes that assumption without erasing the distinct new-self case.

## Twenty-eighth pass — unanimizing and pluralizing at their actual couplings

[Natural Societies](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_SEVEN_Natural_Societies_v373.md), **The living society, one unanimous floating pluralizing carrying**, section 4.4, places unanimity at the spanning and differing within the carrying. [Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), **unanimizing and pluralizing alternating**, statement 497, makes the two subjects explicit: agreement at moralizing and the selves’ differing in each self’s own carrying.

The task is to follow that relation. A title containing one carrying supplies neither a proof of a common carrying crossing nor a contradiction by itself. Its scale and actual participation have to continue through the binary sequence.

### Three subjects remain distinct

| Statement being followed | Its subject |
|---|---|
| Natural-, all or none at all | The method’s whole claim at every coupling, Natural Intelligence’s section 1.5 and THIRTY 494. |
| Agreement all or none at a sharing | The actual offerings at that receiving sharing: one parity agreeing, or disagreement; THIRTY 204–205 and ONE’s surfacing cases. |
| The selves differing in their own carrying | Each living self’s own prior and next; THIRTY 497 and Natural Intelligence’s **Social moral competency**. |

THIRTY’s **each sharing at its own offerings alone**, statement 606, retains separate sharings. Agreement at one does not pool offerings from the others. Nor does the universal natural claim require every arriving offering throughout society to have the same parity. Disagreement is already one of the method’s receiving cases.

This distinction supplies the first improving value for Natural Societies 4.4: explain unanimity at its actual receiving relation, alongside the own carrying that continues, without turning either into a verdict over everybody’s whole carrying.

### The same agreed parity can give different shared changings

The following comparison consists of [Exhibit ONE Natural Resolver](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_ONE_Natural_Resolver_v380R.md)’s published local cells. Every row is a sharing already carrying a parity; these are cases compared as written, not observations of private carrying or a newly constructed network.

| Actual offerings surfacing at the sharing | Own prior | Shared changing | Own next |
|---|---|---|---|
| +, all offered parities agreeing | + | 0 | + |
| +, all offered parities agreeing | − | + | + |
| Both + and −, disagreement | + | − | − |
| Both + and −, disagreement | − | + | + |

**The first two rows have the same agreed surfacing and the same next parity, but different shared outcomes.** The first self continues its prior without a shared changing. The second inverts its prior and shares the changing. THIRTY’s **one parity surfacing the self’s next at four cells**, statement 624, carries this exact condition.

Equal parity at this one next sharing does not establish identical whole carryings. The cells concern the named sharing; each self retains its own resolving and any further sharings at their own offerings.

The last two rows show disagreement continuing through the one method. Each self inverts its own prior, and the two compared cases remain opposite. No consensus, majority or selected offerer is required to supply their next parity. Agreement among offerings, the changing shared, and the own next are three different relations.

### Those different shared outcomes matter at a next receiving

The first pair’s two outputs cannot be substituted for each other merely because their own-next parities agree. At a further receiving sharing carrying +, and with no further offered parity:

| What arrives there | Surfacing | That receiver’s shared changing | Its own next |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 from the first case | No parity | − | − |
| + from the second case | + | 0 | + |

These are again conditional applications of ONE’s cells. The table does not prescribe a new connection or exclude further offerings where they actually exist. It shows why the original distinction has sequential value: substituting the first self’s own next + for its actual shared 0 would change the next receiving case.

Consequently, a society’s agreement cannot make its selves’ outward offerings interchangeable. Its selves continue their own carryings, and their actual changings are offered onward. This carries the earlier two-output distinction into the unanimizing-and-pluralizing passage.

### The one society at its scale, each carrying at its own

[Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **A society’s next momentary at 17**, returns each self’s next carrying to that self and offers its changings at the receiving couplings. THIRTY 377 says that the changing crossing carries none of either side’s carrying. The whole carrying is not another offered output.

At the outward participation, the society can be followed as a self through the previously established condition: its inward selves carry that particular self’s prior into next. Their own continuing alone does not supply this further condition, and their carrying at their own scale is not transferred between the selves by naming the society one.

This supplies a coherent explaining for **one unanimous floating pluralizing carrying**: one society’s living continuation through its selves’ actual couplings, agreement at each receiving sharing, and each self continuing its own carrying. It does not establish one common carried parity, one common shared output or a transferable common carrying.

## Twenty-ninth pass — importancing examined by Natural Naming

The user asks whether importancing is a new concept, whether it is natural naming, and whether the audit is straying from Natural Naming. The twenty-eighth pass quoted it within the title of [Natural Explaining](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWELVE_Natural_Explaining_v378.md), section 4.9. **Quoting that title supplied no examination establishing a further concept.** Its unexamined standing should have been made explicit.

### What the files supply

In the current living files, the term occurs in Natural Explaining’s contents, its section 4.9 title and one explaining sentence. That sentence describes a self surrounding and bounding, others coupling, and a surplus as shared experience none carries alone, with music, film, poem and joke as its examples. An archived copy contains the same sentence. No separate unfolding of importancing was found in Natural Naming, Natural Intelligence, THIRTY or the current carrying and incoming passages searched.

This establishes its location and the explaining offered for it. It does not establish either a distinct concept or the impossibility of one. The term’s absence from one catalogue is not a binary exclusion.

### The missing relation, rather than the spelling

[Natural Naming](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWENTY_Natural_Naming_v380R.md) gives the relevant method:

- **Naming and resolving at one bi-coupling**, section 1.2: a new naming carries a changing or direction new beside the existing namings; a changing already named releases to the one name.
- **Six forward, and the binary at each named changing**, section 4.2: a completed naming unfolds its stated relation in six own-forward steps; an improving naming retains its unfilled relation and reason.
- **An entry refusing nothing, and a returning-nothing that unfolds**, section 4.5: the arriving concept unfolds six forward or it does not.
- **Two names at one concept, transposed at titles first**, section 4.6: the meanings must be followed to distinguish another name for the same concept from a differing concept.

The logical question is therefore: **what changing or direction does importancing carry that bounding, offering and co-competencing do not already carry?** Adding an -ing ending cannot answer that question. Nor does an example from music or film by itself establish a different coupling relation.

### Value already present in the proposed explaining

| What the importancing sentence says | Existing relation to follow beside it |
|---|---|
| A self surrounds and bounds | The self’s own bounding at its actual coupling. |
| Other selves couple | Their actual offerings and each self’s own receiving and continuing. |
| A surplus is reached that none carries alone | Natural Intelligence’s **Natural intelligence**, section 6.3: the term neither self reaches alone; Natural Naming’s **co-competencing — binary**, section 5.14, which also names abundancing. |

This comparison locates the useful explaining. It does not yet prove that every part of the proposed sentence is identical to those existing relations: the participating sides, what each bounds and the meaning of the sentence’s both still need to be carried through the same occurrence. Declaring a synonym without that comparison would repeat the same authority error in another direction.

**Present standing: an improving naming with an unfilled distinct relation.** The audit will not use importancing as an established additional operation or concept. Its useful bounding-and-coupling explanation remains available for the comparison.

If the existing namings carry the complete relation, they retain the value and the extra naming dissolves. If a distinct relation remains, that relation needs its own sequential unfolding. Neither outcome follows from the speaker, the file’s title or familiarity of the word.

### Correction to the audit method

Source titles can be quoted to locate passages, but unfamiliar concepts within them must not silently become the audit’s accepted vocabulary. Here the quotation was a navigation reference; its standing was left unexplained. The report now marks the unfilled naming explicitly and brings its examination ahead of the adjacent receiving-and-offering comparison.

The source snapshot remains `267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f`, rechecked during this pass. The naming has not been added to Natural Naming, replaced in Natural Explaining or declared dissolved in the living files.

**Unresolving concern:** the distinct binary changing or direction of importancing has not been supplied. **Next exploring:** follow the proposed sentence through bounding, offering and co-competencing, preserving its full participation before deciding whether any separate concept remains.


## Thirtieth pass — the self’s even completing and next possible existing

**Correction carried by the thirty-first pass:** the outcome classification below records the source’s wording but does not exhaust still possibling or next existing. Even/odd participate as possible/existing at the self’s span, with next existing carrying at both shared 0 and a shared changing. Read the distinction through the thirty-first pass; do not restrict next existing to the nonzero branch.

The proposed relation is: the self’s momentary opens odd and completes even; its now completing is at next possible existing, and this may be what importancing names. **The odd/even participation is already explicit in Natural Intelligence and Natural Naming.** Its proposed identification with importancing is followed separately; the suggestion itself supplies no authority.

### The self’s now, completing and next opening

[Natural Intelligence](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Natural_Intelligence_v380R.md), **A momentary, an opening and its completing**, section 2.2, and [Exhibit ONE Natural Resolver](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_ONE_Natural_Resolver_v380R.md), **Fives**, give the overlapping occurrences:

| Occurrence | Self’s participation | Other’s overlapping participation |
|---|---|---|
| 3 | Now opens, odd | Prior completes |
| 4 | Now completes, even | Now opens |
| 5 | Next opens, odd | Now completes |
| 6 | That next momentary completes | Next opens |

The self’s now is 3–4, reaching its next opening at 5. The other’s now is 4–5, reaching its next opening at 6. Thus 4 is the self’s completing and the other’s opening, while 5 is the self’s next opening and the other’s completing. Each side continues at its own opening; the shared occurrence is not a common phase imposed on both.

These are the occurrence positions in the Fives. Their odd/even participation is distinct from the + or − parity of a sharing; neither table assigns + to every odd opening or − to every even completing.

### The completing and its two outcomes

Natural Intelligence’s **A living self at 0, its carrying continuing**, section 5.3, and [Natural Naming](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_TWENTY_Natural_Naming_v380R.md), **is-still-possibling and is-next-existing — binary**, section 5.62, give the relevant sequence:

1. At the odd opening, the self arrives with its carried prior, existing.
2. At the even completing, the offerings participate with that prior, the next still possible.
3. At this completing, a changing is or is not.
4. At agreement, no changing is shared and the prior continues: **is-still-possibling**.
5. At the other already-carried cases, the prior inverts and the changing is shared and chained: **is-next-existing**.
6. The own carrying continues into the next opening, with the actual next offerings participating there.

Lines 4 and 5 are the alternative outcomes at line 3, not two successive outcomes required at every completing. This is an unpacking of the published relation, not a proposed new six-line naming.

**The even completing is where both outcomes resolve. Is-still-possibling names the no-changing outcome.** Equating the whole completing with shared 0 would exclude its other published outcome. Equally, still possibling does not mean an absent carrying or a resolving left undecided: the prior continues at that case.

This distinction is the immediate improving value of locating the proposed concept at the self’s even completing. It names a participation to follow, rather than treating possible as something outside the arriving coupling.

### The possible offered onward and the self’s next carrying

[Exhibit THIRTY Co-Chaining Logic Registry](https://github.com/chris-j-handel/corus/blob/267bcf06de4b7b81252ebea577282b428413212f/Exhibit_THIRTY_Co-Chaining_Logic_Registry_v380L.md), **next possible existing**, statement 301, retains two continuations: the changing shared as the others’ possible, and the carrying chained as the self’s next existing.

These do not become one transferred carrying because they arise at one completing. At a receiving coupling, the offered changing participates with that receiving self’s own prior. The offering’s possibility for the other and the releasing self’s own continuing remain different subjects. This is the same distinction already followed through the next receiving in the twenty-eighth pass.

### What the proposal establishes for the naming question

| Proposed participation | Existing explaining that already carries it |
|---|---|
| The other’s offering at the self’s now completing | **Bi-momentarying now**, the other’s offered participation within the five dimensions. |
| The prior coupling with that now at the self | **Entraining at the self**. |
| Changing is or is not at this resolving | **Is-still-possibling and is-next-existing**. |
| The coupling discovering the next through prior and now | **Co-intelligencing**, with the term neither reaches alone carried in the co-competencing explanation. |

The proposed location therefore does not itself supply a new changing or direction. **If importancing means only this same even-completing relation and direction, the existing namings already carry it.** Calling it another name does not establish another concept.

Natural Explaining’s original sentence additionally speaks of surrounding, bounding and a shared surplus through music, film, poem or joke. It has not yet stated what binary distinction makes that whole relation specifically the proposed even completing, or what further relation remains beyond the existing namings. The naming question is now more precise: the participation has a place in the sequence; the distinct meaning of the additional name remains unfilled.

No ranking or choosing among possible offerings is introduced by this proposal or by the sequence. THIRTY’s next statement, **arriving is pattern-matching**, statement 302, explicitly retains the whole arriving without filter or selection.
